\subsection{Input handoffs, proof dependencies and independent review}

\textbf{Input handoffs are not assumed theorems.} The packet IDs listed beneath an assignment identify related source outputs to inspect. They form an acyclic input graph. A packet can start from the cited source proofs, locally derive the needed identity, or analyze a narrower statement when a prior output is absent. It does not wait for every upstream research goal to be solved. A refuted input is evidence for a repair or an obstruction; it cannot be used as a true lemma.

\textbf{Actual proofs have a separate dependency graph.} Each result records every claim it uses by ID, exact statement version or hash, hypotheses, units and evidence state. The registry's initially empty \texttt{proof\_dependencies} fields are templates, not assertions of dependency-free proofs. A full theorem may not depend on an open, refuted or silently strengthened statement. A conditional theorem states and retains its unpaid hypothesis. Changes to an ancestor trigger review of every dependent conclusion; cycles through the target claim are rejected.

The two reviewer IDs attached to each packet are routing suggestions. They do not establish that a review was performed. A referee must read the complete source, reconstruct the decisive steps and record the first unreconstructed implication. The actual reviewer must be distinct from the contributor and must recuse from an independent review of their own argument. Audit outputs receive external challenge through the opposite audit stream and certificate adversary. Every full-FEI candidate still requires eligible, independent reconstructions from \emph{both} B18 and B19; B17's certificate audit does not replace either. If a stream helped author the candidate, another eligible reviewer in that stream is required and acceptance remains pending until that review exists.

\subsection{Completion, partial progress and retirement}

The criterion beneath each packet is its completion contract. A substantive but incomplete derivation can satisfy the work expected from a run while leaving the packet open. Completion of a diagnostic can be a fully scoped refutation; completion of a theorem packet requires its stated proof or an explicitly accepted narrower theorem. Rewriting an equivalent endpoint, replaying a known test, or increasing the page count does not complete a mathematical packet.

After three consecutive runs on the same unpaid obligation with no mathematical delta, the B owner records the exact stuck line and changes method, hypothesis, test family or nearest lemma. These rules neither abandon a scheduled slot nor create more workers, change recurrence counts, reset the campaign or restore an obsolete mission.

For J, record the class and the constant bounding the \emph{complete} residual $\Rcost/\I$. For R, record the full path-plus-leaf cost divided by root influence and the selection theorem making it available. For Booleanity or reveal work, name the exact conditional/history term newly paid. For class theorems, exhibit a growing family outside the old hypotheses. For a full proof, all quantifiers in \eqref{eq:full} and all dependency leaves must be closed. For a disproof, $H(f_m)/I(f_m)\to\infty$ must hold for actual Boolean functions.

\section{One hundred concrete research packets}

Each packet below has a distinct output. Every output must contain a full statement, all hypotheses, a proof or precise gap, original source locators, and a next action. A counterexample packet must also give a reproducible Boolean definition and certify the inequality it violates. The corresponding B-stream has ownership of its five packets, avoiding five independent rediscoveries of the same argument.

The synthesis stream should check progress at least at each natural invocation, but numerical quotas are not success criteria. If a difficult lemma needs several cycles, record its exact latest derivation and unresolved line. Repetition becomes useful only when a new idea, a stronger hypothesis, a different proof, or a new falsifier is introduced.

\subsection{Rules for stopping an unproductive route}

\begin{enumerate}
\item If a known Boolean counterexample violates the proposed helper, stop using that helper immediately. Retain the failed statement and its certificate; attempt a repair only with a stated new hypothesis.
\item If an arbitrary Boolean seed embeds in a proposed simplified class while preserving the target ratio, label the endpoint FEI-equivalent. It can still be pursued, but it is not counted as a reduction in difficulty without a new estimate.
\item If a proof works for arbitrary spectral probability laws but such laws have a known counterexample, locate the missing Boolean constraint before continuing.

Days 6--8 & Concentrate proof effort on the strongest two to four surviving mechanisms; complete a substantial class theorem when possible. & Explicit constants, no unproved representation theorem hidden in a lemma, and complete treatment of difficult endpoints. \\

\subsection{Rules for stopping an unproductive route}

\begin{enumerate}
\item If a known Boolean counterexample violates the proposed helper, stop using that helper immediately. Retain the failed statement and its certificate; attempt a repair only with a stated new hypothesis.
\item If an arbitrary Boolean seed embeds in a proposed simplified class while preserving the target ratio, label the endpoint FEI-equivalent. It can still be pursued, but it is not counted as a reduction in difficulty without a new estimate.
\item If a proof works for arbitrary spectral probability laws but such laws have a known counterexample, locate the missing Boolean constraint before continuing.
\item If successive cycles only rename the same residual or add another equivalent potential, redirect some capacity to a concrete restricted theorem or a different mechanism.

~\mop_fei100\reports\fei-2026-10-11\verification\concurrent_revision_a1ff935\FEI_100_agent_research_plan.tex:1107
\item If an arbitrary Boolean seed embeds in a proposed simplified class while preserving the target ratio, label the endpoint FEI-equivalent. It can still be pursued, but it is not counted as a reduction in difficulty without a new estimate.
\item If a proof works for arbitrary spectral probability laws but such laws have a known counterexample, locate the missing Boolean constraint before continuing.
\item If successive cycles only rename the same residual or add another equivalent potential, redirect some capacity to a concrete restricted theorem or a different mechanism.
\item If a finite computation is the only evidence for a universal statement, require a finite-to-infinite theorem or lower the claim to the exact tested domain.
\end{enumerate}

These are scientific allocation rules. They do not authorize overriding access restrictions, manufacturing a tool result, or abandoning a scheduled slot. A slot with a blocked action should continue independent permitted mathematical work and preserve the actual blocker.

\section{What would count as a significant result}

\subsection{Primary outcome: the unrestricted theorem or a rigorous disproof}

A full proof must close \eqref{eq:full} for every Boolean function and every dimension with one finite constant. It must contain its representation, selection, coding, or structural lemmas as proved statements, including any existence argument. A disproof must construct actual Boolean functions $f_m$ with $H(f_m)/I(f_m)\to\infty$. Exceeding one proposed constant or falsifying an auxiliary spectral inequality does not meet that standard.

\subsection{Major partial outcomes worth completing}

\item If successive cycles only rename the same residual or add another equivalent potential, redirect some capacity to a concrete restricted theorem or a different mechanism.
\item If a finite computation is the only evidence for a universal statement, require a finite-to-infinite theorem or lower the claim to the exact tested domain.
\end{enumerate}

These are scientific allocation rules. They do not authorize overriding access restrictions, manufacturing a tool result, or abandoning a scheduled slot. A slot with a blocked action should continue independent permitted mathematical work and preserve the actual blocker.

\section{What would count as a significant result}

\subsection{Primary outcome: the unrestricted theorem or a rigorous disproof}

\subsection{Major partial outcomes worth completing}

\begin{itemize}

~\mop_fei100\reports\fei-2026-10-11\verification\concurrent_revision_a1ff935\FEI_100_agent_research_plan.tex:1115
\section{What would count as a significant result}

\subsection{Primary outcome: the unrestricted theorem or a rigorous disproof}

A full proof must close \eqref{eq:full} for every Boolean function and every dimension with one finite constant. It must contain its representation, selection, coding, or structural lemmas as proved statements, including any existence argument. A disproof must construct actual Boolean functions $f_m$ with $H(f_m)/I(f_m)\to\infty$. Exceeding one proposed constant or falsifying an auxiliary spectral inequality does not meet that standard.

\subsection{Major partial outcomes worth completing}

\begin{itemize}
\item A universal good-coordinate erasure inequality, or a globally summable stopping-tree theorem reaching known terminal classes. Either closes a precise route to full FEI.
\item A bound on the whole-witness residual for a class with genuinely unbounded overlap that lies outside the existing bounded-read or orthogonal-multiplexer hypotheses. State exactly how the new parameter improves the old logarithmic loss.
\item An arity-uniform product-biased corrected FEI+ theorem for arbitrary weighted thresholds, with its resulting depth-independent read-once formula consequence.
\item A Boolean-specific conditional entropy or phase-energy theorem under a proved stable structural condition, together with a clear account of the remaining class outside that condition.
\item A full FEI theorem for another broad, currently unresolved family, supported by a primary-source novelty check. Do not assert that a family is new merely because it is absent from one local manuscript.
\item A rigorous scalable counterfamily that rules out a major live universal helper, or a corrected sharp auxiliary theorem replacing it. Such a result can save substantial research effort without being a disproof of FEI.
\end{itemize}

~\mop_fei100\reports\fei-2026-10-11\verification\concurrent_revision_a1ff935\FEI_100_agent_research_plan.tex:1122

\begin{itemize}
\item A universal good-coordinate erasure inequality, or a globally summable stopping-tree theorem reaching known terminal classes. Either closes a precise route to full FEI.
\item A bound on the whole-witness residual for a class with genuinely unbounded overlap that lies outside the existing bounded-read or orthogonal-multiplexer hypotheses. State exactly how the new parameter improves the old logarithmic loss.
\item An arity-uniform product-biased corrected FEI+ theorem for arbitrary weighted thresholds, with its resulting depth-independent read-once formula consequence.
\item A Boolean-specific conditional entropy or phase-energy theorem under a proved stable structural condition, together with a clear account of the remaining class outside that condition.

\end{itemize}

Improving $8.937$ to a slightly smaller number, running more random examples of an already proved identity, or producing longer exposition without a new mathematical implication is a lower priority during this campaign. These can be useful after a major theorem is secure, but they should not displace work on the unpaid global terms.

\section{Coordination, artifacts, and mathematical acceptance}

\subsection{A durable record for every invocation}

Every invocation first reads the campaign's current status, its own most recent complete result, the relevant peer handoffs, and the accessible Brain inbox. It then works on one concrete mathematical increment in its assigned lane. At the end, it saves the complete derivation and any useful partial result, counterexample, failed attempt, computation, or precise blocked step. A status-only acknowledgment is not the substantive output of a research run.

Use a unique run directory so concurrent writers do not overwrite one another. The full research route in the private open-problems repository is
\begin{quote}\small
\url{https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/tree/master/reports/fei-2026-10-11}
\end{quote}
with runs under \texttt{runs/Bxx/<UTC\_RUN\_ID>/}. The complete plan is also saved in the Brain repository at
\begin{quote}\small

~\mop_fei100\reports\fei-2026-10-11\verification\concurrent_revision_a1ff935\FEI_100_agent_research_plan.tex:1155
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{Target:} exact statement, units, domain, hypotheses, and intended implication for FEI.
\item \textbf{Inputs:} source files actually read, immutable revisions, theorem/page locators, and the exact prior open step.
\item \textbf{Result:} full proof, counterexample, or explicitly incomplete derivation. State the first remaining unsupported line.
\item \textbf{Validation:} original commands, code, deterministic seeds where used, exit status, tested domain, error bounds, and actual outputs. Mark unexecuted work as such.
\item \textbf{Dependencies:} accepted lemmas, unverified lemmas, falsified candidates, and whether this result changes any upstream conclusion.
\item \textbf{Handoff:} one next mathematical action with its expected deliverable and a distinct proposed reviewer.
\item \textbf{Persistence:} actual repository commit and paths, plus any unresolved save, delivery, or access failure.
\end{enumerate}

The adjacent machine-readable \texttt{AGENT\_ASSIGNMENTS.json}, schema 2, retains all one hundred original A-packets and B owners and supplies their input handoffs, completion criteria, reviewer routes and F-topic connections. Section~\ref{sec:operational} defines how these contracts are used. The \texttt{CURRENT\_STATUS.md} file is maintained by B20 as the concise navigation layer; it never replaces the original proofs. Optimistic branch updates should use the observed parent revision and preserve unrelated concurrent changes. Failed writes do not count as saved artifacts.

\subsection{Acceptance states}

Use distinct states for a proposed claim, an analytically proved claim, a finite verified statement, an independently reviewed theorem, and a public release. A finite computational check can verify a finite statement exactly; it cannot by itself promote an all-dimensional theorem. A source index or repository upload does not imply native Brain ingestion or downstream use.


~\mop_fei100\reports\fei-2026-10-11\verification\concurrent_revision_a1ff935\FEI_100_agent_research_plan.tex:1167
\subsection{Acceptance states}

Use distinct states for a proposed claim, an analytically proved claim, a finite verified statement, an independently reviewed theorem, and a public release. A finite computational check can verify a finite statement exactly; it cannot by itself promote an all-dimensional theorem. A source index or repository upload does not imply native Brain ingestion or downstream use.

For a complete full-FEI candidate, both audit streams must inspect the complete dependency chain and the known obstruction suite. They should independently reconstruct the critical inequalities and check all limit passages. A proof-assistant certificate is valuable when available, but absence of one is not replaced by assertions of formal verification. The author decides dissemination of future results; the present authorization publishes the recovered paper and keeps this plan and campaign research in the requested private repositories.

\section{First actions ready to execute}

The following packets can begin immediately from the recovered material, without waiting for a new universal idea:
\begin{enumerate}
\item \textbf{B02:} retain nonempty-root conditioning and posterior witness entropy in a complete computation for an iterated or shared-data selector family. Compare the resulting residual with the original coordinatewise bound.
\item \textbf{B06:} derive an adaptive-block analogue of the good-coordinate erasure criterion and prove its exact influence telescope. Test it on a coded restriction plateau before generalizing.
\item \textbf{B09:} isolate the first use of symmetry in the biased-threshold proof that fails for unequal weights. Produce either a replacement probability lemma or an explicit obstruction to that replacement.
\item \textbf{B07/B16:} start from the genuine Boolean phase-energy example, preserve the full conditional entropy, and test a precisely stated correlated-auxiliary improvement. Include the arbitrary-tensor embedding as a mandatory scope check.
\item \textbf{B12:} use the exact shared-data collision identity to prove a class theorem beyond the established orthogonal-neighbor and uniform-positive-selector-energy cases. Target vanishing selector-energy fractions, controlled selector reuse, or another explicitly new growing family.
\item \textbf{B15/B17:} choose one current unproved helper and produce an exact bounded countersearch with a certified logarithmic comparison for any found violation. State the search domain in advance.

~\mop_fei100\reports\fei-2026-10-11\verification\concurrent_revision_a1ff935\FEI_100_agent_research_plan.tex:1179
\item \textbf{B09:} isolate the first use of symmetry in the biased-threshold proof that fails for unequal weights. Produce either a replacement probability lemma or an explicit obstruction to that replacement.
\item \textbf{B07/B16:} start from the genuine Boolean phase-energy example, preserve the full conditional entropy, and test a precisely stated correlated-auxiliary improvement. Include the arbitrary-tensor embedding as a mandatory scope check.
\item \textbf{B12:} use the exact shared-data collision identity to prove a class theorem beyond the established orthogonal-neighbor and uniform-positive-selector-energy cases. Target vanishing selector-energy fractions, controlled selector reuse, or another explicitly new growing family.
\item \textbf{B15/B17:} choose one current unproved helper and produce an exact bounded countersearch with a certified logarithmic comparison for any found violation. State the search domain in advance.
\end{enumerate}

The governing question for each work cycle is: \emph{Which exact unpaid mathematical term became bounded, which unjustified hypothesis was eliminated, or which plausible route was rigorously ruled out?} Keeping that answer tied to original proofs and actual artifacts gives the 100-agent design its value.

\section*{Preparation note}
This report was prepared during recovery of the interrupted 11 October 2026 FEI work using OpenAI's GPT-6 Astra Pro with collaborating agents. It combines source-grounded analysis of the recovered manuscripts, accessible Brain notes, primary literature, independent mathematical audits, and explicit proposed research tasks. The A001--A100 assignments are a deployment design; they are not a claim that one hundred external Codex agents were launched. The scheduler verification record separately identifies the twenty existing tasks actually reconfigured. This operational revision independently compared the installed roster with the reconciled F-role supplement, checked the B05 analytic no-go scope, and added the one hundred completion/dependency/reviewer contracts. It does not claim execution or completion of those packets.
